You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"663c301f-fa73-4243-9a53-cc7f13c5b6e5","uuid":"9125a8c8-b273-433e-9073-b1517ef07d91","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/eduardo.torres/Downloads/COE Operation Nexus/.agent-studio/worktrees/electron-architect-t2/src/hub/ParticleNetwork.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  radius: number;\n  color: string;\n}\n\nconst PARTICLE_COLORS = [\n  'rgba(59,130,246,',   // blue\n  'rgba(139,92,246,',   // purple\n  'rgba(16,185,129,',   // green\n];\n\nconst CONNECTION_DISTANCE = 180;\nconst PARTICLE_COUNT = 80;\n\nfunction createParticles(width: number, height: number): Particle[] {\n  return Array.from({ length: PARTICLE_COUNT }, () => {\n    const colorBase = PARTICLE_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * PARTICLE_COLORS.length)];\n    return {\n      x: Math.random() * width,\n      y: Math.random() * height,\n      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,\n      vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,\n      radius: Math.random() * 1.5 + 1,\n      color: colorBase,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\ninterface ParticleNetworkProps {\n  isDark: boolean;\n}\n\nexport default function ParticleNetwork({ isDark }: ParticleNetworkProps) {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const animFrameRef = useRef<number>(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    function resize() {\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      canvas.width = canvas.offsetWidth * window.devicePixelRatio;\n      canvas.height = canvas.offsetHeight * window.devicePixelRatio;\n      ctx!.scale(window.devicePixelRatio, window.devicePixelRatio);\n      particlesRef.current = createParticles(canvas.offsetWidth, canvas.offsetHeight);\n    }\n\n    resize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);\n\n    const nodeOpacity = isDark ? 0.7 : 0.5;\n    const lineOpacity = isDark ? 0.4 : 0.25;\n\n    function animate() {\n      if (!canvas || !ctx) return;\n\n      const w = canvas.offsetWidth;\n      const h = canvas.offsetHeight;\n      const particles = particlesRef.current;\n\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);\n\n      for (const p of particles) {\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        if (p.x < 0 || p.x > w) p.vx *= -1;\n        if (p.y < 0 || p.y > h) p.vy *= -1;\n\n        p.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(w, p.x));\n        p.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(h, p.y));\n      }\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particles.length; j++) {\n          const dx = particles[i].x - particles[j].x;\n          const dy = particles[i].y - particles[j].y;\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n          if (dist < CONNECTION_DISTANCE) {\n            const opacity = lineOpacity * (1 - dist / CONNECTION_DISTANCE);\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(particles[i].x, particles[i].y);\n            ctx.lineTo(particles[j].x, particles[j].y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = particles[i].color + String(opacity) + ')';\n            ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      for (const p of particles) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color + String(nodeOpacity) + ')';\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n    }\n\n    animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameRef.current);\n    };\n  }, [isDark]);\n\n  return (\n    <canvas\n      ref={canvasRef}\n      className=\"absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none z-0\"\n      aria-hidden=\"true\"\n    />\n  );\n}\n","numLines":129,"startLine":1,"totalLines":129}}}

resume.education.length > 0\n      ? `\n  <section class=\"section\">\n    <h2 class=\"section-title\">Education</h2>\n    ${resume.education\n      .map(\n        (edu) => `\n      <div class=\"education-item\">\n        <div class=\"education-header\">\n          <div>\n            <span class=\"degree\">${edu.degree} in ${edu.field}</span>\n            <span class=\"institution\"> | ${edu.institution}</span>\n            ${edu.gpa ? `<span style=\"font-size: 10pt; color: #6b7280;\"> | GPA: ${edu.gpa}</span>` : ''}\n          </div>\n          <span class=\"dates\">${formatDate(edu.graduationDate)}</span>\n        </div>\n        ${edu.honors ? `<p style=\"font-size: 10pt; color: #6b7280; margin-top: 2px;\">${edu.honors}</p>` : ''}\n      </div>\n    `\n      )\n      .join('')}\n  </section>\n  `\n      : ''\n  }\n\n  ${\n    resume.skills.length > 0\n      ? `\n  <section class=\"section\">\n    <h2 class=\"section-title\">Skills</h2>\n    <div class=\"skills-container\">\n      ${resume.skills\n        .map(\n          (cat) => `\n        <div class=\"skill-category\">\n          <div class=\"skill-category-name\">${cat.name}</div>\n          <div class=\"skills-list\">${cat.skills.join(' • ')}</div>\n        </div>\n      `\n        )\n        .join('')}\n    </div>\n  </section>\n  `\n      : ''\n  }\n\n  ${\n    resume.certifications.length > 0\n      ? `\n  <section class=\"section\">\n    <h2 class=\"section-title\">Certifications</h2>\n    <div class=\"certifications-list\">\n      ${resume.certifications\n        .map(\n          (cert) => `\n        <div class=\"certification-item\">\n          <div>\n            <span class=\"cert-name\">${cert.name}</span>\n            <span class=\"cert-issuer\"> - ${cert.issuer}</span>\n          </div>\n          <span class=\"cert-date\">${formatDate(cert.date)}</span>\n        </div>\n      `\n        )\n        .join('')}\n    </div>\n  </section>\n  `\n      : ''\n  }\n\n  <footer class=\"footer\">\n    Generated with Resume Transformer | © ${new Date().getFullYear()} {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-20250514","id":"msg_01Qx7LSG53xMHEfSNVS2LSgM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011Ftsx9yDrrEXybBfFZxDpE","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eduardo.torres/Downloads/COE Operation Nexus/.agent-studio/worktrees/electron-architect-t2/electron/main/index.ts","content":"import { app, BrowserWindow, shell, ipcMain, session } from 'electron';\nimport { join } from 'path';\nimport express from 'express';\nimport { createServer } from 'http';\n\nconst isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';\nconst RENDERER_DIST = join(__dirname, '../dist');\nconst VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL = process.env.VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL;\n\nlet mainWindow: BrowserWindow | null = null;\nlet expressServer: ReturnType<typeof createServer> | null = null;\nconst EXPRESS_PORT = 5002;\n\nfunction createMainWindow() {\n  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({\n    width: 1200,\n    height: 800,\n    minWidth: 1024,\n    minHeight: 768,\n    show: false,\n    icon: join(__dirname, '../../resources/icon.png'),\n    titleBarStyle: process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'hiddenInset' : 'default',\n    webPreferences: {\n      contextIsolation: true,\n      nodeIntegration: false,\n      sandbox: true,\n      webSecurity: true,\n      preload: join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),\n    },\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      shell.openExternal(url);\n    }\n    return { action: 'deny' };\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navigationUrl) => {\n    const parsedUrl = new URL(navigationUrl);\n\n    if (parsedUrl.origin !== new URL(mainWindow!.webContents.getURL()).origin) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      shell.openExternal(navigationUrl);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL) {\n    mainWindow.loadURL(VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL);\n    mainWindow.webContents.openDevTools();\n  } else {\n    mainWindow.loadFile(join(RENDERER_DIST, 'index.html'));\n  }\n\n  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {\n    mainWindow?.show();\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {\n    mainWindow = null;\n  });\n\n  return mainWindow;\n}\n\nasync function startExpressServer() {\n  const expressApp = express();\n\n  expressApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }));\n  expressApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '50mb' }));\n\n  expressApp.use((req, res, next) => {\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Origin, X-Requested-With, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization');\n\n    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {\n      res.sendStatus(200);\n    } else {\n      next();\n    }\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sync/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sync endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/processing/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Processing endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/match/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Match endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/database/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Database endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sessions/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sessions endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    expressServer = expressApp.listen(EXPRESS_PORT, () => {\n      console.log(`Express server running on http://localhost:${EXPRESS_PORT}`);\n      resolve();\n    }).on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupSecurityHeaders() {\n  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {\n    callback({\n      responseHeaders: {\n        ...details.responseHeaders,\n        'Content-Security-Policy': [\n          \"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' data: blob: http://localhost:* ws://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"object-src 'none'; \" +\n          \"base-uri 'self';\"\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction registerIpcHandlers() {\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getVersion', () => {\n    return app.getVersion();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getPlatform', () => {\n    return process.platform;\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:minimize', () => {\n    mainWindow?.minimize();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:maximize', () => {\n    if (mainWindow?.isMaximized()) {\n      mainWindow.unmaximize();\n    } else {\n      mainWindow?.maximize();\n    }\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:close', () => {\n    mainWindow?.close();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('shell:openExternal', async (event, url: string) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      await shell.openExternal(url);\n      return { success: true };\n    }\n\n    return { success: false, error: 'Protocol not allowed' };\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupAppHandlers() {\n  app.whenReady().then(async () => {\n    setupSecurityHeaders();\n    registerIpcHandlers();\n\n    try {\n      await startExpressServer();\n      console.log('Express server started successfully');\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to start Express server:', error);\n    }\n\n    createMainWindow();\n\n    app.on('activate', () => {\n      if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {\n        createMainWindow();\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {\n    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {\n      if (expressServer) {\n        expressServer.close(() => {\n          console.log('Express server closed');\n        });\n      }\n      app.quit();\n    }\n  });\n\n  app.on('before-quit', () => {\n    if (expressServer) {\n      expressServer.close();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nif (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {\n  app.quit();\n} else {\n  app.on('second-instance', () => {\n    if (mainWindow) {\n      if 'hiddenInset' : 'default',\n    webPreferences: {\n      contextIsolation: true,\n      nodeIntegration: false,\n      sandbox: true,\n      webSecurity: true,\n      preload: join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),\n    },\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      shell.openExternal(url);\n    }\n    return { action: 'deny' };\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navigationUrl) => {\n    const parsedUrl = new URL(navigationUrl);\n\n    if (parsedUrl.origin !== new URL(mainWindow!.webContents.getURL()).origin) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      shell.openExternal(navigationUrl);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL) {\n    mainWindow.loadURL(VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL);\n    mainWindow.webContents.openDevTools();\n  } else {\n    mainWindow.loadFile(join(RENDERER_DIST, 'index.html'));\n  }\n\n  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {\n    mainWindow?.show();\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {\n    mainWindow = null;\n  });\n\n  return mainWindow;\n}\n\nasync function startExpressServer() {\n  const expressApp = express();\n\n  expressApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }));\n  expressApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '50mb' }));\n\n  expressApp.use((req, res, next) => {\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Origin, X-Requested-With, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization');\n\n    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {\n      res.sendStatus(200);\n    } else {\n      next();\n    }\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sync/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sync endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/processing/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Processing endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/match/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Match endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/database/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Database endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sessions/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sessions endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    expressServer = expressApp.listen(EXPRESS_PORT, () => {\n      console.log(`Express server running on http://localhost:${EXPRESS_PORT}`);\n      resolve();\n    }).on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupSecurityHeaders() {\n  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {\n    callback({\n      responseHeaders: {\n        ...details.responseHeaders,\n        'Content-Security-Policy': [\n          \"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' data: blob: http://localhost:* ws://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"object-src 'none'; \" +\n          \"base-uri 'self';\"\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction registerIpcHandlers() {\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getVersion', () => {\n    return app.getVersion();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getPlatform', () => {\n    return process.platform;\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:minimize', () => {\n    mainWindow?.minimize();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:maximize', () => {\n    if (mainWindow?.isMaximized()) {\n      mainWindow.unmaximize();\n    } else {\n      mainWindow?.maximize();\n    }\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:close', () => {\n    mainWindow?.close();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('shell:openExternal', async (event, url: string) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      await shell.openExternal(url);\n      return { success: true };\n    }\n\n    return { success: false, error: 'Protocol not allowed' };\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupAppHandlers() {\n  app.whenReady().then(async () => {\n    setupSecurityHeaders();\n    registerIpcHandlers();\n\n    try {\n      await startExpressServer();\n      console.log('Express server started successfully');\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to start Express server:', error);\n    }\n\n    createMainWindow();\n\n    app.on('activate', () => {\n      if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {\n        createMainWindow();\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {\n    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {\n      if (expressServer) {\n        expressServer.close(() => {\n          console.log('Express server closed');\n        });\n      }\n      app.quit();\n    }\n  });\n\n  app.on('before-quit', () => {\n    if (expressServer) {\n      expressServer.close();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nif (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {\n  app.quit();\n} else {\n  app.on('second-instance', () => {\n    if (mainWindow) {\n      if error?: string }>;\n  };\n}\n\nconst electronAPI: ElectronAPI = {\n  app: {\n    getVersion: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:getVersion'),\n    getPlatform: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:getPlatform'),\n  },\n  window: {\n    minimize: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('window:minimize'),\n    maximize: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('window:maximize'),\n    close: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('window:close'),\n  },\n  shell: {\n    openExternal: (url: string) => ipcRenderer.invoke('shell:openExternal', url),\n  },\n};\n\nif (process.contextIsolated) {\n  try {\n    contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('electronAPI', electronAPI);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Failed to expose electronAPI:', error);\n  }\n} else {\n  (window as any).electronAPI = electronAPI;\n}\n\nwindow.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  console.log('Preload script loaded successfully');\n});\n\ndeclare global {\n  interface Window {\n    electronAPI: ElectronAPI;\n  }\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":7,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2181,"cache_read_input_tokens":66918,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2181},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"663c301f-fa73-4243-9a53-cc7f13c5b6e5","uuid":"5497bc58-cc5a-4070-9c87-ec88a4e1b30e"}

'hiddenInset' : 'default',\n    webPreferences: {\n      contextIsolation: true,\n      nodeIntegration: false,\n      sandbox: true,\n      webSecurity: true,\n      preload: join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),\n    },\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      shell.openExternal(url);\n    }\n    return { action: 'deny' };\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navigationUrl) => {\n    const parsedUrl = new URL(navigationUrl);\n\n    if (parsedUrl.origin !== new URL(mainWindow!.webContents.getURL()).origin) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      shell.openExternal(navigationUrl);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL) {\n    mainWindow.loadURL(VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL);\n    mainWindow.webContents.openDevTools();\n  } else {\n    mainWindow.loadFile(join(RENDERER_DIST, 'index.html'));\n  }\n\n  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {\n    mainWindow?.show();\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {\n    mainWindow = null;\n  });\n\n  return mainWindow;\n}\n\nasync function startExpressServer() {\n  const expressApp = express();\n\n  expressApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }));\n  expressApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '50mb' }));\n\n  expressApp.use((req, res, next) => {\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Origin, X-Requested-With, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization');\n\n    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {\n      res.sendStatus(200);\n    } else {\n      next();\n    }\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sync/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sync endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/processing/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Processing endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/match/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Match endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/database/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Database endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sessions/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sessions endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    expressServer = expressApp.listen(EXPRESS_PORT, () => {\n      console.log(`Express server running on http://localhost:${EXPRESS_PORT}`);\n      resolve();\n    }).on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupSecurityHeaders() {\n  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {\n    callback({\n      responseHeaders: {\n        ...details.responseHeaders,\n        'Content-Security-Policy': [\n          \"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' data: blob: http://localhost:* ws://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"object-src 'none'; \" +\n          \"base-uri 'self';\"\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction registerIpcHandlers() {\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getVersion', () => {\n    return app.getVersion();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getPlatform', () => {\n    return process.platform;\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:minimize', () => {\n    mainWindow?.minimize();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:maximize', () => {\n    if (mainWindow?.isMaximized()) {\n      mainWindow.unmaximize();\n    } else {\n      mainWindow?.maximize();\n    }\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:close', () => {\n    mainWindow?.close();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('shell:openExternal', async (event, url: string) 'path';\nimport express from 'express';\nimport { createServer } from 'http';\nimport { IPC_CHANNELS } from '../shared/ipc-channels';\n\nconst isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';\nconst RENDERER_DIST = join(__dirname, '../dist');\nconst VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL = process.env.VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL;\n\nlet mainWindow: BrowserWindow | null = null;\nlet expressServer: ReturnType<typeof createServer> | null = null;\nconst EXPRESS_PORT = 5002;\n\nfunction createMainWindow() {\n  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({\n    width: 1200,\n    height: 800,\n    minWidth: 1024,\n    minHeight: 768,\n    show: false,\n    icon: join(__dirname, '../../resources/icon.png'),\n    titleBarStyle: process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'hiddenInset' : 'default',\n    webPreferences: {\n      contextIsolation: true,\n      nodeIntegration: false,\n      sandbox: true,\n      webSecurity: true,\n      preload: join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),\n    },\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      shell.openExternal(url);\n    }\n    return { action: 'deny' };\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navigationUrl) => {\n    const parsedUrl = new URL(navigationUrl);\n\n    if (parsedUrl.origin !== new URL(mainWindow!.webContents.getURL()).origin) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      shell.openExternal(navigationUrl);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL) {\n    mainWindow.loadURL(VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL);\n    mainWindow.webContents.openDevTools();\n  } else {\n    mainWindow.loadFile(join(RENDERER_DIST, 'index.html'));\n  }\n\n  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {\n    mainWindow?.show();\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {\n    mainWindow = null;\n  });\n\n  return mainWindow;\n}\n\nasync function startExpressServer() {\n  const expressApp = express();\n\n  expressApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }));\n  expressApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '50mb' }));\n\n  expressApp.use((req, res, next) => {\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Origin, X-Requested-With, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization');\n\n    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {\n      res.sendStatus(200);\n    } else {\n      next();\n    }\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sync/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sync endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/processing/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Processing endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/match/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Match endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/database/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Database endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sessions/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sessions endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    expressServer = expressApp.listen(EXPRESS_PORT, () => {\n      console.log(`Express server running on http://localhost:${EXPRESS_PORT}`);\n      resolve();\n    }).on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupSecurityHeaders() {\n  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {\n    callback({\n      responseHeaders: {\n        ...details.responseHeaders,\n        'Content-Security-Policy': [\n          \"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' data: blob: http://localhost:* ws://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"object-src 'none'; \" +\n          \"base-uri 'self';\"\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction registerIpcHandlers() {\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getVersion', () => {\n    return app.getVersion();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('app:getPlatform', () => {\n    return process.platform;\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:minimize', () => {\n    mainWindow?.minimize();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:maximize', () => {\n    if (mainWindow?.isMaximized()) {\n      mainWindow.unmaximize();\n    } else {\n      mainWindow?.maximize();\n    }\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('window:close', () => {\n    mainWindow?.close();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle('shell:openExternal', async (event, url: string) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      await shell.openExternal(url);\n      return { success: true };\n    }\n\n    return { success: false, error: 'Protocol not allowed' };\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupAppHandlers() {\n  app.whenReady().then(async () => {\n    setupSecurityHeaders();\n    registerIpcHandlers();\n\n    try {\n      await startExpressServer();\n      console.log('Express server started successfully');\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to start Express server:', error);\n    }\n\n    createMainWindow();\n\n    app.on('activate', () => {\n      if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {\n        createMainWindow();\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {\n    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {\n      if (expressServer) {\n        expressServer.close(() => {\n          console.log('Express server closed');\n        });\n      }\n      app.quit();\n    }\n  });\n\n  app.on('before-quit', () => {\n    if (expressServer) {\n      expressServer.close();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nif (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {\n  app.quit();\n} else {\n  app.on('second-instance', () => {\n    if (mainWindow) {\n      if (mainWindow.isMinimized()) mainWindow.restore();\n      mainWindow.focus();\n    }\n  });\n\n express from 'express';\nimport { createServer } from 'http';\nimport { IPC_CHANNELS } from '../shared/ipc-channels';\n\nconst isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';\nconst RENDERER_DIST = join(__dirname, '../dist');\nconst VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL = process.env.VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL;\n\nlet mainWindow: BrowserWindow | null = null;\nlet expressServer: ReturnType<typeof createServer> | null = null;\nconst EXPRESS_PORT = 5002;\n\nfunction createMainWindow() {\n  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({\n    width: 1200,\n    height: 800,\n    minWidth: 1024,\n    minHeight: 768,\n    show: false,\n    icon: join(__dirname, '../../resources/icon.png'),\n    titleBarStyle: process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'hiddenInset' : 'default',\n    webPreferences: {\n      contextIsolation: true,\n      nodeIntegration: false,\n      sandbox: true,\n      webSecurity: true,\n      preload: join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),\n    },\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      shell.openExternal(url);\n    }\n    return { action: 'deny' };\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navigationUrl) => {\n    const parsedUrl = new URL(navigationUrl);\n\n    if (parsedUrl.origin !== new URL(mainWindow!.webContents.getURL()).origin) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      shell.openExternal(navigationUrl);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL) {\n    mainWindow.loadURL(VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL);\n    mainWindow.webContents.openDevTools();\n  } else {\n    mainWindow.loadFile(join(RENDERER_DIST, 'index.html'));\n  }\n\n  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {\n    mainWindow?.show();\n  });\n\n  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {\n    mainWindow = null;\n  });\n\n  return mainWindow;\n}\n\nasync function startExpressServer() {\n  const expressApp = express();\n\n  expressApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }));\n  expressApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '50mb' }));\n\n  expressApp.use((req, res, next) => {\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');\n    res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Origin, X-Requested-With, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization');\n\n    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {\n      res.sendStatus(200);\n    } else {\n      next();\n    }\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sync/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sync endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/processing/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Processing endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/match/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Match endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/database/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Database endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  expressApp.get('/api/sessions/*', (req, res) => {\n    res.json({ message: 'Sessions endpoint - implement backend logic here' });\n  });\n\n  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    expressServer = expressApp.listen(EXPRESS_PORT, () => {\n      console.log(`Express server running on http://localhost:${EXPRESS_PORT}`);\n      resolve();\n    }).on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupSecurityHeaders() {\n  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {\n    callback({\n      responseHeaders: {\n        ...details.responseHeaders,\n        'Content-Security-Policy': [\n          \"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' data: blob: http://localhost:* ws://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:*; \" +\n          \"object-src 'none'; \" +\n          \"base-uri 'self';\"\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction registerIpcHandlers() {\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.APP.GET_VERSION, () => {\n    return app.getVersion();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.APP.GET_PLATFORM, () => {\n    return process.platform;\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.WINDOW.MINIMIZE, () => {\n    mainWindow?.minimize();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.WINDOW.MAXIMIZE, () => {\n    if (mainWindow?.isMaximized()) {\n      mainWindow.unmaximize();\n    } else {\n      mainWindow?.maximize();\n    }\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.WINDOW.CLOSE, () => {\n    mainWindow?.close();\n  });\n\n  ipcMain.handle(IPC_CHANNELS.SHELL.OPEN_EXTERNAL, async (event, url: string) => {\n    const allowedProtocols = ['https:', 'http:', 'mailto:'];\n    const urlObject = new URL(url);\n\n    if (allowedProtocols.includes(urlObject.protocol)) {\n      await shell.openExternal(url);\n      return { success: true };\n    }\n\n    return { success: false, error: 'Protocol not allowed' };\n  });\n}\n\nfunction setupAppHandlers() {\n  app.whenReady().then(async () => {\n    setupSecurityHeaders();\n    registerIpcHandlers();\n\n    try {\n      await startExpressServer();\n      console.log('Express server started successfully');\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to start Express server:', error);\n    }\n\n    createMainWindow();\n\n    app.on('activate', () => {\n      if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {\n        createMainWindow();\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {\n    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {\n      if (expressServer) {\n        expressServer.close(() => {\n          console.log('Express server closed');\n        });\n      }\n      app.quit();\n    }\n  });\n\n  app.on('before-quit', () => {\n    if (expressServer) {\n      expressServer.close();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nif (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {\n  app.quit();\n} else
